+func TestSupervisedStepRejectsAgentRunnerExecutionTarget(t *testing.T) {
+	step := Step{ID: "supervised-one", Kind: "code", Mode: SupervisedBlackBox, HandoffPolicy: supervisedHandoffPolicy()}
+	facts := &AgentRunnerImmutableFacts{RequestID: "request-one"}
+	if err := (StepExecutionIntent{ExecutionTarget: AgentRunnerExecution, AgentRunnerFacts: facts}).validate(step); !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidDefinition) {
+		t.Fatalf("a supervised step must never accept an AgentRunner execution target, got %v", err)
+	}
+	if err := (StepExecutionIntent{ExecutionTarget: DefaultExecution, AgentRunnerFacts: facts}).validate(step); !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidDefinition) {
+		t.Fatalf("a supervised step must never carry AgentRunner facts, got %v", err)
+	}
+	// Positive control: the same step with the default target is routable.
+	if err := (StepExecutionIntent{ExecutionTarget: DefaultExecution}).validate(step); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("a supervised step with the default target must validate: %v", err)
+	}
+}
+
+// requireKeys pins the exact key set of a marshalled record to the frozen
+// schema, including the additionalProperties:false contract.
+func requireKeys(t *testing.T, fields map[string]any, want []string) {
+	t.Helper()
+	if len(fields) != len(want) {
+		t.Fatalf("key count %d, want %d: %v", len(fields), len(want), fields)
+	}
+	for _, key := range want {
+		if _, ok := fields[key]; !ok {
+			t.Fatalf("missing key %q in %v", key, fields)
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func marshalFields(t *testing.T, value any) map[string]any {
+	t.Helper()
+	payload, err := json.Marshal(value)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	var fields map[string]any
+	if err := json.Unmarshal(payload, &fields); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	return fields
+}
+
+func nestedFields(t *testing.T, fields map[string]any, key string) map[string]any {
+	t.Helper()
+	nested, ok := fields[key].(map[string]any)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("%q is not an object: %v", key, fields)
+	}
+	return nested
+}
+
+func TestSupervisedRecordJSONShapesMatchFrozenSchemas(t *testing.T) {
+	confirmation := marshalFields(t, supervisedConfirmation(t, SupervisedRiskNoticeVersion, SupervisedRiskNoticeHash()))
+	requireKeys(t, confirmation, []string{"schemaVersion", "record", "recordHash"})
+	requireKeys(t, nestedFields(t, confirmation, "record"), []string{
+		"kind", "recordId", "issuedAt", "issuedBy", "runId", "taskId", "stepId", "attempt",
+		"workspaceHash", "riskNoticeVersion", "riskNoticeHash", "selection", "delivery",
+		"interactionEvidence", "evidence",
+	})
+	requireKeys(t, nestedFields(t, nestedFields(t, confirmation, "record"), "delivery"), []string{
+		"channel", "deliveredAt", "deliveryStatus", "transportRequestId", "errorEvidence",
+	})
+	requireKeys(t, nestedFields(t, nestedFields(t, confirmation, "record"), "issuedBy"), []string{"type", "id"})
+
+	report, err := NewSupervisedReport(SupervisedReportInput{
+		ReportID:           "supervised-report-one",
+		RunID:              "run-one",
+		TaskID:             "task-one",
+		StepID:             "supervised-one",
+		Attempt:            1,
+		VerifiedDimensions: []string{"independent-gates"},
+		UnknownDimensions:  []string{"cost"},
+		Evidence:           []string{supervisedReportEvidence},
+	}, fixedClock, func() string { return "supervised-report-one" })
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	record := marshalFields(t, report)
+	requireKeys(t, record, []string{"schemaVersion", "record", "recordHash"})
+	requireKeys(t, nestedFields(t, record, "record"), []string{
+		"kind", "reportId", "recordedAt", "recordedBy", "runId", "taskId", "stepId", "attempt",
+		"mode", "assuranceLevel", "verifiedDimensions", "unknownDimensions", "evidence",
+	})
+	requireKeys(t, nestedFields(t, nestedFields(t, record, "record"), "recordedBy"), []string{"type", "id"})
+}
+
+func TestRouterRefusesSupervisedWithoutPort(t *testing.T) {
+	request := StepRequest{RunID: "run-one", TaskID: "task-one", Step: Step{ID: "supervised-one", Kind: "code", Mode: SupervisedBlackBox}, Attempt: 1}
+	if _, err := (StepRouter{}).Execute(context.Background(), request); !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidDefinition) {
+		t.Fatalf("a supervised step without its port must be refused, got %v", err)
+	}
+	recorder := &supervisedRouteRecorder{requests: nil}
+	router := StepRouter{Managed: &routeRecorder{}, Isolated: &routeRecorder{}, Handoff: &routeRecorder{}, Supervised: recorder, Hooks: &routeRecorder{}}
+	if _, err := router.Execute(context.Background(), request); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("a supervised step must route to its own port: %v", err)
+	}
+	if recorder.calls != 1 || len(recorder.requests) != 1 || recorder.requests[0].Step.Mode != SupervisedBlackBox {
+		t.Fatalf("the supervised port was not the sole route: %+v", recorder)
+	}
+}
+
+// supervisedConfirmationWithDelivery re-freezes a valid confirmation whose
+// delivery fact carries the given status. The record shape permits the
+// unavailable/unknown statuses; the window opener is the place that must refuse
+// them.
+func supervisedConfirmationWithDelivery(t *testing.T, status string) SupervisedRiskConfirmationRecord {
+	t.Helper()
+	record := supervisedConfirmation(t, SupervisedRiskNoticeVersion, SupervisedRiskNoticeHash())
+	record.Record.Delivery.DeliveryStatus = status
+	hash, err := digestRecord(supervisedRiskConfirmationDomain, record.Record)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	record.RecordHash = hash
+	if err := ValidateSupervisedRiskConfirmation(record); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("a %s delivery fact must still be a well-formed record: %v", status, err)
+	}
+	return record
+}
+
+// TestOpenSupervisedWorkWindowRejectsUnacceptedDelivery pins the delivery-status
+// invariant of contracts section 7.1a at the window-opening entry: a
+// confirmation whose delivery was not accepted must be refused with zero writes,
+// even though the record itself is well-formed.
+func TestOpenSupervisedWorkWindowRejectsUnacceptedDelivery(t *testing.T) {
+	for _, status := range []string{"unavailable", "unknown"} {
+		t.Run(status, func(t *testing.T) {
+			fixture := newSupervisedFixture(t)
+			fixture.driveToRunning(t)
+			events := fixture.eventCount(t)
+			err := fixture.engine.OpenSupervisedWorkWindow(context.Background(), fixture.session, supervisedConfirmationWithDelivery(t, status))
+			if !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidSupervisedConfirmation) {
+				t.Fatalf("a non-accepted delivery must be refused at the window opener, got %v", err)
+			}
+			if fixture.eventCount(t) != events {
+				t.Fatal("the refused open wrote state")
+			}
+			fixture.requireStates(t, "RUNNING", "STEPS_RUNNING", "PAUSED")
+		})
+	}
+}
+
+// TestSupervisedRiskNoticeIsTruthfulAndVersioned pins the F-4 contract of the
+// versioned risk notice: the text must name the current notice version, and it
+// must not claim an OS-level isolation boundary ProofRail does not build.
+func TestSupervisedRiskNoticeIsTruthfulAndVersioned(t *testing.T) {
+	if !strings.Contains(SupervisedRiskNoticeText, fmt.Sprintf("version %d", SupervisedRiskNoticeVersion)) {
+		t.Fatalf("the notice text must name the current version %d: %q", SupervisedRiskNoticeVersion, SupervisedRiskNoticeText)
+	}
+	if strings.Contains(SupervisedRiskNoticeText, "OS-isolated") {
+		t.Fatalf("the notice must not claim an OS-level isolation boundary it does not build: %q", SupervisedRiskNoticeText)
+	}
+	if !strings.Contains(SupervisedRiskNoticeText, "not an OS-level sandbox") {
+		t.Fatalf("the notice must state the absence of OS-level isolation: %q", SupervisedRiskNoticeText)
+	}
+}
diff --git a/internal/chain/supervised_report.go b/internal/chain/supervised_report.go
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..90bfbcd
--- /dev/null
+++ b/internal/chain/supervised_report.go
@@ -0,0 +1,212 @@
+package chain
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"slices"
+
+	"github.com/larsonzh/prfrail/internal/evidence"
+)
+
+// The reduced/unknown report is the factual record of what a supervised
+// black-box run can and cannot prove (contracts section 7.1d). The assurance
+// level is permanently "reduced": it is the ceiling of the mode, never a
+// degraded state. The unknown dimension set is non-empty, always contains
+// "cost", and every member comes from the frozen closed set, so an unknown
+// dimension can never be rendered as safe.
+const (
+	supervisedReportDomain     = "proofrail:supervised-report:1\n"
+	supervisedReportKind       = "supervised-report"
+	supervisedReportSchema     = "1.0.0"
+	supervisedReportMode       = "supervised-black-box"
+	supervisedAssuranceReduced = "reduced"
+	supervisedUnknownCost      = "cost"
+)
+
+// supervisedUnknownDimensionSet is the frozen closed set of dimensions that are
+// unobservable for an external agent.
+var supervisedUnknownDimensionSet = []string{
+	"tool-use",
+	"resource-reads",
+	"network-access",
+	"cost",
+	"background-processes",
+	"session-continuity",
+	"external-side-effects",
+}
+
+// supervisedVerifiedDimensionSet is the frozen closed set of dimensions
+// ProofRail proves about its own obligations.
+var supervisedVerifiedDimensionSet = []string{
+	"workspace-isolation",
+	"manifest-before",
+	"manifest-after",
+	"diff",
+	"scope-scan",
+	"secret-scan",
+	"file-type-scan",
+	"side-effect-scan",
+	"process-stop-proof",
+	"independent-gates",
+	"independent-review",
+}
+
+// SupervisedReport is the report body. AssuranceLevel is not an input field:
+// the constructor always sets the reduced constant.
+type SupervisedReport struct {
+	Kind               string         `json:"kind"`
+	ReportID           string         `json:"reportId"`
+	RecordedAt         string         `json:"recordedAt"`
+	RecordedBy         evidence.Actor `json:"recordedBy"`
+	RunID              string         `json:"runId"`
+	TaskID             string         `json:"taskId"`
+	StepID             string         `json:"stepId"`
+	Attempt            int            `json:"attempt"`
+	Mode               string         `json:"mode"`
+	AssuranceLevel     string         `json:"assuranceLevel"`
+	VerifiedDimensions []string       `json:"verifiedDimensions"`
+	UnknownDimensions  []string       `json:"unknownDimensions"`
+	Evidence           []string       `json:"evidence"`
+}
+
+// SupervisedReportRecord is the persisted envelope.
+type SupervisedReportRecord struct {
+	SchemaVersion string           `json:"schemaVersion"`
+	Record        SupervisedReport `json:"record"`
+	RecordHash    string           `json:"recordHash"`
+}
+
+// SupervisedReportInput is the caller-supplied body of a new report.
+type SupervisedReportInput struct {
+	ReportID           string
+	RecordedAt         string
+	RunID              string
+	TaskID             string
+	StepID             string
+	Attempt            int
+	VerifiedDimensions []string
+	UnknownDimensions  []string
+	Evidence           []string
+}
+
+// NewSupervisedReport builds and validates a report. The assurance level is
+// always the reduced constant, and the unknown dimension set always contains
+// "cost": a caller can list fewer unobservable dimensions, never fewer than the
+// mode demands, and can never raise the level.
+func NewSupervisedReport(input SupervisedReportInput, clock Clock, ids IDSource) (SupervisedReportRecord, error) {
+	if clock == nil {
+		clock = defaultClock
+	}
+	if ids == nil {
+		ids = randomID
+	}
+	unknown := cloneStrings(input.UnknownDimensions)
+	if len(unknown) == 0 {
+		unknown = cloneStrings(supervisedUnknownDimensionSet)
+	}
+	if !slices.Contains(unknown, supervisedUnknownCost) {
+		unknown = append(unknown, supervisedUnknownCost)
+	}
+	body := SupervisedReport{
+		Kind:               supervisedReportKind,
+		ReportID:           input.ReportID,
+		RecordedAt:         input.RecordedAt,
+		RecordedBy:         evidence.Actor{Type: "system", ID: "proofrail"},
+		RunID:              input.RunID,
+		TaskID:             input.TaskID,
+		StepID:             input.StepID,
+		Attempt:            input.Attempt,
+		Mode:               supervisedReportMode,
+		AssuranceLevel:     supervisedAssuranceReduced,
+		VerifiedDimensions: cloneStrings(input.VerifiedDimensions),
+		UnknownDimensions:  unknown,
+		Evidence:           cloneStrings(input.Evidence),
+	}
+	if body.ReportID == "" {
+		body.ReportID = ids()
+	}
+	if body.RecordedAt == "" {
+		body.RecordedAt = clock().UTC().Format(supervisedTimeLayout)
+	}
+	hash, err := digestRecord(supervisedReportDomain, body)
+	if err != nil {
+		return SupervisedReportRecord{}, err
+	}
+	record := SupervisedReportRecord{SchemaVersion: supervisedReportSchema, Record: body, RecordHash: hash}
+	if err := ValidateSupervisedReport(record); err != nil {
+		return SupervisedReportRecord{}, err
+	}
+	return record, nil
+}
+
+// DecodeSupervisedReport strictly decodes and validates one report.
+func DecodeSupervisedReport(input []byte) (SupervisedReportRecord, error) {
+	var record SupervisedReportRecord
+	if err := evidence.DecodeStrictJSON(input, &record); err != nil {
+		return SupervisedReportRecord{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: %v", ErrInvalidSupervisedReport, err)
+	}
+	if err := ValidateSupervisedReport(record); err != nil {
+		return SupervisedReportRecord{}, err
+	}
+	return record, nil
+}
+
+// ValidateSupervisedReport checks the envelope and the body, and recomputes the
+// canonical record hash. A report that claims full assurance, omits "cost", or
+// lists a dimension outside the closed set is refused.
+func ValidateSupervisedReport(record SupervisedReportRecord) error {
+	if record.SchemaVersion != supervisedReportSchema || !evidence.ValidHash(record.RecordHash) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: schema version or record hash", ErrInvalidSupervisedReport)
+	}
+	if err := validateSupervisedReportBody(record.Record); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	expected, err := digestRecord(supervisedReportDomain, record.Record)
+	if err != nil || expected != record.RecordHash {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: recordHash mismatch", ErrInvalidSupervisedReport)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func validateSupervisedReportBody(body SupervisedReport) error {
+	if body.Kind != supervisedReportKind {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: invalid kind", ErrInvalidSupervisedReport)
+	}
+	if !evidence.ValidID(body.ReportID) || !evidence.ValidID(body.RunID) || !evidence.ValidID(body.TaskID) || !evidence.ValidID(body.StepID) || body.Attempt < 1 {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: invalid binding identity", ErrInvalidSupervisedReport)
+	}
+	if body.RecordedBy.Type != "system" || !evidence.ValidID(body.RecordedBy.ID) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: invalid recording actor", ErrInvalidSupervisedReport)
+	}
+	if !validSupervisedTimestamp(body.RecordedAt) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: invalid record time", ErrInvalidSupervisedReport)
+	}
+	if body.Mode != supervisedReportMode {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: invalid mode", ErrInvalidSupervisedReport)
+	}
+	if body.AssuranceLevel != supervisedAssuranceReduced {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: assurance level is permanently reduced", ErrInvalidSupervisedReport)
+	}
+	if len(body.UnknownDimensions) == 0 || !slices.Contains(body.UnknownDimensions, supervisedUnknownCost) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: unknown dimensions must be non-empty and carry cost", ErrInvalidSupervisedReport)
+	}
+	for _, dimension := range body.UnknownDimensions {
+		if !slices.Contains(supervisedUnknownDimensionSet, dimension) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("%w: unknown dimension %q is outside the closed set", ErrInvalidSupervisedReport, dimension)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(uniqueHashes(body.UnknownDimensions)) != len(body.UnknownDimensions) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: unknown dimensions must be unique", ErrInvalidSupervisedReport)
+	}
+	for _, dimension := range body.VerifiedDimensions {
+		if !slices.Contains(supervisedVerifiedDimensionSet, dimension) {
+			return fmt.Errorf("%w: verified dimension %q is outside the closed set", ErrInvalidSupervisedReport, dimension)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(uniqueHashes(body.VerifiedDimensions)) != len(body.VerifiedDimensions) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: verified dimensions must be unique", ErrInvalidSupervisedReport)
+	}
+	if !validUniqueHashSet(body.Evidence, true) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: report evidence must be a non-empty unique hash set", ErrInvalidSupervisedReport)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
diff --git a/internal/chain/supervised_risk_confirmation.go b/internal/chain/supervised_risk_confirmation.go
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..febb3a0
--- /dev/null
+++ b/internal/chain/supervised_risk_confirmation.go
@@ -0,0 +1,288 @@
+package chain
+
+import (
+	"errors"
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"time"
+
+	"github.com/larsonzh/prfrail/internal/evidence"
+)
+
+// The binding risk confirmation is the start-up precondition of the
+// supervised-black-box mode (contracts section 7.1a). It fixes run/task/step/
+// attempt, the isolated-workspace digest, the risk-notice version and hash, the
+// visible delivery fact and the request/response interaction evidence. Its
+// selection is permanently "confirm": a rejection is never a confirmation, and
+// a notice version or hash that drifts from the current constants is refused
+// before any work window opens.
+const (
+	supervisedRiskConfirmationDomain = "proofrail:supervised-risk-confirmation:1\n"
+	supervisedRiskConfirmationKind   = "supervised-risk-confirmation"
+	supervisedRiskConfirmationSchema = "1.0.0"
+	supervisedVisibleChannel         = "sessionbridge-visible"
+	supervisedTimeLayout             = "2006-01-02T15:04:05.000Z"
+	// supervisedDeliveryAccepted is the only delivery status that may open a
+	// work window (contracts section 7.1a). The record shape also permits
+	// "unavailable" and "unknown" so a delivery fact can be recorded honestly,
+	// but the window opener refuses both.
+	supervisedDeliveryAccepted = "accepted"
+)
+
+var (
+	// ErrInvalidSupervisedConfirmation reports a risk confirmation that cannot
+	// be trusted: a wrong schema version or hash, a malformed binding, a
+	// selection other than "confirm", an illegal delivery fact or malformed
+	// evidence.
+	ErrInvalidSupervisedConfirmation = errors.New("invalid supervised risk confirmation")
+	// ErrInvalidSupervisedReport reports a reduced/unknown report that cannot be
+	// trusted: a wrong assurance level, an unknown dimension outside the frozen
+	// closed set, an empty or cost-free unknown set, or a malformed hash.
+	ErrInvalidSupervisedReport = errors.New("invalid supervised report")
+	// supervisedTransportIDPattern is the accepted transport request id shape.
+	// It is deliberately broader than evidence.ValidID because the visible
+	// transport id is owned by SessionBridge, not by the ProofRail id grammar.
+	supervisedTransportIDPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,127}$`)
+)
+
+// SupervisedDelivery is the factual record of one visible delivery attempt. It
+// carries no completion claim: the delivery only proves that a notice was
+// handed to the operator UI, never that any work happened.
+type SupervisedDelivery struct {
+	Channel            string   `json:"channel"`
+	DeliveredAt        string   `json:"deliveredAt"`
+	DeliveryStatus     string   `json:"deliveryStatus"`
+	TransportRequestID *string  `json:"transportRequestId"`
+	ErrorEvidence      []string `json:"errorEvidence"`
+}
+
+// SupervisedRiskConfirmation is the record body. It is exported so callers can
+// read the binding, but it must always be built and validated as a whole.
+type SupervisedRiskConfirmation struct {
+	Kind                string             `json:"kind"`
+	RecordID            string             `json:"recordId"`
+	IssuedAt            string             `json:"issuedAt"`
+	IssuedBy            evidence.Actor     `json:"issuedBy"`
+	RunID               string             `json:"runId"`
+	TaskID              string             `json:"taskId"`
+	StepID              string             `json:"stepId"`
+	Attempt             int                `json:"attempt"`
+	WorkspaceHash       string             `json:"workspaceHash"`
+	RiskNoticeVersion   int                `json:"riskNoticeVersion"`
+	RiskNoticeHash      string             `json:"riskNoticeHash"`
+	Selection           string             `json:"selection"`
+	Delivery            SupervisedDelivery `json:"delivery"`
+	InteractionEvidence []string           `json:"interactionEvidence"`
+	Evidence            []string           `json:"evidence"`
+}
+
+// SupervisedRiskConfirmationRecord is the persisted envelope. The canonical
+// record hash domain separator is applied by the hash layer, never by the
+// stored JSON.
+type SupervisedRiskConfirmationRecord struct {
+	SchemaVersion string                     `json:"schemaVersion"`
+	Record        SupervisedRiskConfirmation `json:"record"`
+	RecordHash    string                     `json:"recordHash"`
+}
+
+// SupervisedRiskConfirmationInput is the caller-supplied body of a new
+// confirmation. Selection is not an input: it is always "confirm".
+type SupervisedRiskConfirmationInput struct {
+	RecordID            string
+	IssuedAt            string
+	OperatorID          string
+	RunID               string
+	TaskID              string
+	StepID              string
+	Attempt             int
+	WorkspaceHash       string
+	RiskNoticeVersion   int
+	RiskNoticeHash      string
+	Delivery            SupervisedDelivery
+	InteractionEvidence []string
+	Evidence            []string
+}
+
+// NewSupervisedRiskConfirmation builds and validates a confirmation record. The
+// record hash is recomputed over the canonical body so a caller can never hand
+// the engine a body that does not match its own digest.
+func NewSupervisedRiskConfirmation(input SupervisedRiskConfirmationInput, clock Clock, ids IDSource) (SupervisedRiskConfirmationRecord, error) {
+	if clock == nil {
+		clock = defaultClock
+	}
+	if ids == nil {
+		ids = randomID
+	}
+	body := SupervisedRiskConfirmation{
+		Kind:                supervisedRiskConfirmationKind,
+		RecordID:            input.RecordID,
+		IssuedAt:            input.IssuedAt,
+		IssuedBy:            evidence.Actor{Type: "operator", ID: input.OperatorID},
+		RunID:               input.RunID,
+		TaskID:              input.TaskID,
+		StepID:              input.StepID,
+		Attempt:             input.Attempt,
+		WorkspaceHash:       input.WorkspaceHash,
+		RiskNoticeVersion:   input.RiskNoticeVersion,
+		RiskNoticeHash:      input.RiskNoticeHash,
+		Selection:           "confirm",
+		Delivery:            input.Delivery,
+		InteractionEvidence: cloneStrings(input.InteractionEvidence),
+		Evidence:            cloneStrings(input.Evidence),
+	}
+	if body.RecordID == "" {
+		body.RecordID = ids()
+	}
+	if body.IssuedAt == "" {
+		body.IssuedAt = clock().UTC().Format(supervisedTimeLayout)
+	}
+	if body.Delivery.ErrorEvidence == nil {
+		body.Delivery.ErrorEvidence = []string{}
+	}
+	hash, err := digestRecord(supervisedRiskConfirmationDomain, body)
+	if err != nil {
+		return SupervisedRiskConfirmationRecord{}, err
+	}
+	record := SupervisedRiskConfirmationRecord{SchemaVersion: supervisedRiskConfirmationSchema, Record: body, RecordHash: hash}
+	if err := ValidateSupervisedRiskConfirmation(record); err != nil {
+		return SupervisedRiskConfirmationRecord{}, err
+	}
+	return record, nil
+}
+
+// DecodeSupervisedRiskConfirmation strictly decodes and validates one record.
+func DecodeSupervisedRiskConfirmation(input []byte) (SupervisedRiskConfirmationRecord, error) {
+	var record SupervisedRiskConfirmationRecord
+	if err := evidence.DecodeStrictJSON(input, &record); err != nil {
+		return SupervisedRiskConfirmationRecord{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: %v", ErrInvalidSupervisedConfirmation, err)
+	}
+	if err := ValidateSupervisedRiskConfirmation(record); err != nil {
+		return SupervisedRiskConfirmationRecord{}, err
+	}
+	return record, nil
+}
+
+// ValidateSupervisedRiskConfirmation checks the envelope and the body, and
+// recomputes the canonical record hash so a tampered body can never validate.
+func ValidateSupervisedRiskConfirmation(record SupervisedRiskConfirmationRecord) error {
+	if record.SchemaVersion != supervisedRiskConfirmationSchema || !evidence.ValidHash(record.RecordHash) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: schema version or record hash", ErrInvalidSupervisedConfirmation)
+	}
+	if err := validateSupervisedRiskConfirmationBody(record.Record); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	expected, err := digestRecord(supervisedRiskConfirmationDomain, record.Record)
+	if err != nil || expected != record.RecordHash {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: recordHash mismatch", ErrInvalidSupervisedConfirmation)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func validateSupervisedRiskConfirmationBody(body SupervisedRiskConfirmation) error {
+	if body.Kind != supervisedRiskConfirmationKind {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: invalid kind", ErrInvalidSupervisedConfirmation)
+	}
+	if !evidence.ValidID(body.RecordID) || !evidence.ValidID(body.RunID) || !evidence.ValidID(body.TaskID) || !evidence.ValidID(body.StepID) || body.Attempt < 1 {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: invalid binding identity", ErrInvalidSupervisedConfirmation)
+	}
+	if body.IssuedBy.Type != "operator" || !evidence.ValidID(body.IssuedBy.ID) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: invalid issuing operator", ErrInvalidSupervisedConfirmation)
+	}
+	if !validSupervisedTimestamp(body.IssuedAt) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: invalid issue time", ErrInvalidSupervisedConfirmation)
+	}
+	if !evidence.ValidHash(body.WorkspaceHash) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: invalid workspace digest", ErrInvalidSupervisedConfirmation)
+	}
+	if body.RiskNoticeVersion < 1 || !evidence.ValidHash(body.RiskNoticeHash) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: invalid risk notice binding", ErrInvalidSupervisedConfirmation)
+	}
+	if body.Selection != "confirm" {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: selection must be confirm", ErrInvalidSupervisedConfirmation)
+	}
+	if err := validateSupervisedDelivery(body.Delivery); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	if len(body.InteractionEvidence) != 2 || !validUniqueHashSet(body.InteractionEvidence, true) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: interaction evidence must bind the request and the response", ErrInvalidSupervisedConfirmation)
+	}
+	if !validUniqueHashSet(body.Evidence, true) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: confirmation evidence must be a non-empty unique hash set", ErrInvalidSupervisedConfirmation)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func validateSupervisedDelivery(delivery SupervisedDelivery) error {
+	if delivery.Channel != supervisedVisibleChannel {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: unknown delivery channel", ErrInvalidSupervisedConfirmation)
+	}
+	if !validSupervisedTimestamp(delivery.DeliveredAt) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: invalid delivery time", ErrInvalidSupervisedConfirmation)
+	}
+	switch delivery.DeliveryStatus {
+	case "accepted", "unavailable", "unknown":
+	default:
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: unknown delivery status", ErrInvalidSupervisedConfirmation)
+	}
+	if delivery.TransportRequestID != nil && !supervisedTransportIDPattern.MatchString(*delivery.TransportRequestID) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: invalid transport request id", ErrInvalidSupervisedConfirmation)
+	}
+	if !validUniqueHashSet(delivery.ErrorEvidence, false) {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: invalid delivery error evidence", ErrInvalidSupervisedConfirmation)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func validSupervisedTimestamp(value string) bool {
+	_, err := time.Parse(supervisedTimeLayout, value)
+	return err == nil
+}
+
+// SupervisedRiskConfirmationBinding identifies the step attempt a confirmation
+// belongs to.
+type SupervisedRiskConfirmationBinding struct {
+	RunID   string
+	TaskID  string
+	StepID  string
+	Attempt int
+}
+
+// SupervisedRiskConfirmationLedger is the append-only, no-replace collection of
+// confirmations. Append is idempotent for the same step attempt and the same
+// digest, and refuses a second, divergent confirmation for the same step
+// attempt instead of silently replacing it.
+type SupervisedRiskConfirmationLedger struct {
+	Records []SupervisedRiskConfirmationRecord
+}
+
+// Append validates and records one confirmation.
+func (ledger *SupervisedRiskConfirmationLedger) Append(record SupervisedRiskConfirmationRecord) error {
+	if err := ValidateSupervisedRiskConfirmation(record); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	for _, existing := range ledger.Records {
+		if existing.Record.RunID != record.Record.RunID || existing.Record.TaskID != record.Record.TaskID ||
+			existing.Record.StepID != record.Record.StepID || existing.Record.Attempt != record.Record.Attempt {
+			continue
+		}
+		if existing.RecordHash == record.RecordHash {
+			// Same step attempt, same digest: a replay converges on the record
+			// that is already durable.
+			return nil
+		}
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: a different confirmation already binds this step attempt", ErrInvalidSupervisedConfirmation)
+	}
+	ledger.Records = append(ledger.Records, record)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// Confirmed reports whether a valid confirmation binds the step attempt.
+func (ledger SupervisedRiskConfirmationLedger) Confirmed(binding SupervisedRiskConfirmationBinding) bool {
+	for _, record := range ledger.Records {
+		if record.Record.RunID == binding.RunID && record.Record.TaskID == binding.TaskID &&
+			record.Record.StepID == binding.StepID && record.Record.Attempt == binding.Attempt {
+			return true
+		}
+	}
+	return false
+}

+// anyProvided reports whether at least one AgentRunner input flag was supplied.
+// It is the hazard half of the supervised/AgentRunner mutual exclusion.
+func (inputs agentRunnerRunInputs) anyProvided() bool {
+	for _, value := range []string{
+		inputs.requestRecord,
+		inputs.capabilityRecord,
+		inputs.enforcementRecord,
+		inputs.availabilityRecord,
+		inputs.authorizationLedger,
+		inputs.costLedger,
+		inputs.agentCLI,
+		inputs.agentVersion,
+	} {
+		if strings.TrimSpace(value) != "" {
+			return true
+		}
+	}
+	return false
+}

+// FindSupervisedSteps lists the supervised-black-box steps of a definition. A
+// definition that names at least one such step routes to the supervised
+// assembly instead of the noop-only CLI path.
+func FindSupervisedSteps(cfg ChainConfig) []ExecutableStep {
+	steps := make([]ExecutableStep, 0)
+	for _, task := range cfg.Tasks {
+		for _, step := range task.Steps {
+			if step.Kind == "code" && step.Execution == supervisedExecutionMode {
+				steps = append(steps, ExecutableStep{TaskID: task.ID, StepID: step.ID, Kind: step.Kind})
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	return steps
+}
+
+// validateSupervisedChannel fails closed when a definition declares a supervised
+// step without the AI configuration and the visible channel the mode delivers
+// through. The risk notice must reach an operator UI, so a missing `ai` block or
+// a channel list without `sessionbridge-visible` is refused before any run.
+func validateSupervisedChannel(cfg ChainConfig) error {
+	if len(FindSupervisedSteps(cfg)) == 0 {
+		return nil
+	}
+	if cfg.AI == nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: supervised-black-box steps require an ai configuration with the %s channel", ErrInvalidConfig, supervisedRequiredChannel)
+	}
+	for _, binding := range cfg.AI.Channels {
+		if binding.Channel == supervisedRequiredChannel {
+			return nil
+		}
+	}
+	return fmt.Errorf("%w: supervised-black-box steps require the %s channel", ErrInvalidConfig, supervisedRequiredChannel)
+}
+

+package console

+// This file is the production supervised-black-box assembly of the console
+// package. It adds no chain state and no chain transition: the mode is driven
+// entirely through the existing operator-interaction entries
+// (OpenOperatorInteraction / ResumeOperatorInteraction) plus the supervised
+// window entries (OpenSupervisedWorkWindow / SubmitSupervisedReturn). It never
+// lets a visible delivery, a chat message or an operator confirmation reach a
+// terminal state on its own.
+//
+// # How the run is driven without the step dispatch
+//
+// A supervised step routed through StepRouter.Execute inside engine.Run would
+// make the engine treat the port's ErrAwaitingSupervisedConfirmation sentinel
+// as a step failure (step-failed -> failTask). The assembly therefore parks the
+// run from inside the engine's existing failpoint seam: the failpoint is
+// invoked after the step reached RUNNING and before any dispatch happens, so it
+// can deliver the notice, persist the binding interaction and pause the run
+// while leaving a clean STEPS_RUNNING/RUNNING/PAUSED frontier. engine.Run is
+// only ever used again for the tail chain (postflight -> review -> publish).
+//
+// # Disk state machine
+//
+// Every later invocation decides its phase from the run root alone:
+//
+//	phase A  no interaction request persisted  -> deliver, open the interaction, park
+//	phase B  request persisted, no response    -> waiting for the operator
+//	phase C  response persisted, no confirmation -> confirm, open the work window
+//	phase D  window open, no return receipt    -> waiting for the explicit return
+//	phase E  chain terminal                   -> nothing left to do
+//
+// Phase D is state-aware: a durable confirmation with no persisted session means
+// the process died inside the confirm/resume/open/persist sequence, so the
+// existing confirmation is replayed (never rebuilt) until the window and the
+// session agree. A replay that cannot converge returns a structured error
+// instead of stalling on the non-empty ledger.
+//
+// The residual is the window between persisting the request and writing the
+// pause transition (plan section 9.7): a crash inside it leaves a RUNNING step
+// whose re-entry fails closed as recovery-uncertain. The ordering is
+// persist-then-transition on purpose; PILOT does not claim power-cut atomicity.
+
+// The supervised store-local layout under <runRoot>/supervised/. These files are
+// store-local protocol, not wire records: no schema and no external consumer
+// depends on them.
+const (
+	supervisedDirectoryName          = "supervised"
+	supervisedInteractionsName       = "operator-interactions.jsonl"
+	supervisedConfirmationsName      = "risk-confirmations.jsonl"
+	supervisedDeliveryName           = "delivery.json"
+	supervisedSessionName            = "session.json"
+	supervisedFrozenPolicyName       = "handoff-policy.json"
+	supervisedReceiptName            = "handoff-receipt.json"
+	supervisedReportName             = "report.json"
+	supervisedScansDirectoryName     = "scans"
+	supervisedBeforeManifestName     = "before-manifest.json"
+	supervisedScanReportName         = "scan-report.json"
+	supervisedGateEvidenceName       = "gate-evidence.json"
+	supervisedSnapshotStoreDirectory = "snapshot-store"
+	supervisedWorkspacesDirectory    = "workspaces"
+	supervisedConfirmationResponse   = "confirm"
+	supervisedRejectionResponse      = "reject"
+	supervisedDeliveryAcceptedStatus = "accepted"
+	supervisedStepFailpoint          = "step-running"
+	supervisedAdapterID              = "sessbridge-visible"
+	supervisedOperatorID             = "local-operator"
+	supervisedRequiredActionText     = "supervise the external agent, then run: prfrail supervised return --run-id <id> --outcome complete|abort"
+	supervisedRiskDescription        = "reduced-assurance-external-agent"
+	supervisedReviewPolicyLabel      = "supervised-assembly-policy"
+)
+
+// Console-local evidence domains. They are internal digests, never wire records.
+const (
+	supervisedHandoffPolicyDomain         = "proofrail:supervised-handoff-policy:1\n"

+	supervisedCandidateEvidenceRootDomain = "proofrail:supervised-candidate-evidence-root:1\n"

+)

+// The durable boundaries of the confirmation sequence, in order. A process that
+// dies at one of them leaves a non-empty confirmation ledger with no persisted
+// session; resumeSupervisedRun detects that and converges.
+const (
+	supervisedCrashAfterConfirmation = "after-confirmation"
+	supervisedCrashAfterResume       = "after-resume"
+	supervisedCrashAfterWindowOpen   = "after-window-open"
+)

+// The frozen closed sets mirrored from internal/chain. HAND-SYNCHRONISED
+// COUPLING (unknown set): internal/chain owns the authoritative set
+// (supervisedUnknownDimensionSet); the two copies must stay equal and must be
+// re-evaluated together. The copy exists because the console must build a
+// report without exporting the chain's unexported set; ValidateSupervisedReport
+// still refuses anything outside the chain's own set, so a drift can only ever
+// fail closed.
+//
+// supervisedVerifiedDimensions is deliberately NOT the chain's full closed set:
+// it is the subset proven at the moment the report is frozen. The report is
+// written before the tail chain (postflight -> review -> publish) runs, so
+// "independent-review" is not yet true at write time and is omitted rather than
+// asserted (P4: never present the unproven as proven). The chain's closed set
+// still accepts it, so a later slice may record it once a post-review write
+// point exists.
+var (
+	supervisedUnknownDimensions  = []string{"tool-use", "resource-reads", "network-access", "cost", "background-processes", "session-continuity", "external-side-effects"}
+	supervisedVerifiedDimensions = []string{"workspace-isolation", "manifest-before", "manifest-after", "diff", "scope-scan", "secret-scan", "file-type-scan", "side-effect-scan", "process-stop-proof", "independent-gates"}
+)
+
+var (
+	// errSupervisedParked is the console marker the failpoint returns to park the
+	// run in the waiting state. It is never a step failure.
+	errSupervisedParked = errors.New("supervised run parked awaiting operator confirmation")
+	// ErrSupervisedStepRequired reports a definition that is not exactly one
+	// supervised-black-box code step.
+	ErrSupervisedStepRequired = errors.New("the supervised assembly requires exactly one supervised-black-box code step")
+	// ErrSupervisedDeliveryBlocked reports a visible delivery that was not
+	// accepted. The work window must never open.
+	ErrSupervisedDeliveryBlocked = errors.New("supervised visible delivery is not accepted")
+	// ErrSupervisedRejected reports an operator response that did not confirm.
+	ErrSupervisedRejected = errors.New("supervised risk confirmation rejected")
+	// ErrSupervisedTimeout reports a work window that outlived its frozen handoff
+	// timeout: a complete return is refused and only abort remains possible.
+	ErrSupervisedTimeout = errors.New("supervised handoff window timed out; only abort is permitted")
+	// ErrSupervisedPolicyDrift reports a complete return attempted under a handoff
+	// policy that no longer matches the policy frozen when the work window
+	// opened. A changed chain configuration (or a changed config path) would
+	// otherwise let the return enforce a swapped timeout, authorized scope or
+	// after-return gate set, so the guard fails closed before any receipt, report
+	// or chain transition is written.
+	ErrSupervisedPolicyDrift = errors.New("supervised handoff policy drifted since the window opened")
+	// ErrSupervisedGateUnavailable reports a missing after-return gate runner or
+	// hook definition. The gate is not optional, so the return fails closed.
+	ErrSupervisedGateUnavailable = errors.New("supervised after-return gate is unavailable")
+	// ErrSupervisedGateFailed reports a failed after-return gate hook. A
+	// confirmation never exempts a gate.
+	ErrSupervisedGateFailed = errors.New("supervised after-return gate hook failed")
+	// ErrSupervisedSecretFinding reports credential material in the returning
+	// workspace.
+	ErrSupervisedSecretFinding = errors.New("supervised post-return secret scan found credential material")
+	// ErrSupervisedNotOpen reports a return attempted without a persisted window.
+	ErrSupervisedNotOpen = errors.New("supervised work window is not open")
+	// ErrSupervisedAbortOnly reports an unknown return outcome.
+	ErrSupervisedAbortOnly = errors.New("supervised return outcome must be complete or abort")
+	// ErrSupervisedScopeUnderivable reports an authorized scope that cannot be
+	// derived from the frozen handoff policy (no declared allowed target, or an
+	// unresolvable workspace root). An underivable scope is never read as "no
+	// restriction": it fails closed before any comparison happens.
+	ErrSupervisedScopeUnderivable = errors.New("supervised authorized scope cannot be derived")
+	// ErrSupervisedScopeOutOfBounds reports a post-return change whose path leaves
+	// the authorized write scope. ProofRail has both the duty and the ability to
+	// prove this, so an observed out-of-scope path blocks the return (plan
+	// section 8: detectable => block).
+	ErrSupervisedScopeOutOfBounds = errors.New("supervised post-return change set leaves the authorized scope")
+	// ErrSupervisedSideEffect reports an observable side effect on the isolated
+	// workspace: a deletion of a pre-existing entry, a type change, a
+	// permission/executable change, or a link whose target leaves the workspace
+	// root.
+	ErrSupervisedSideEffect = errors.New("supervised post-return side effect detected")
+	// ErrSupervisedFileType reports an entry type the return scan does not accept.
+	ErrSupervisedFileType = errors.New("supervised post-return file type is not accepted")
+	// ErrSupervisedWindowUnproven reports a re-entry that carries a durable
+	// confirmation but neither an open window nor a replayable resume: the run
+	// cannot converge and the operator must inspect the run manually.
+	ErrSupervisedWindowUnproven = errors.New("supervised confirmed window could not be reopened")
+)
